Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the atoms space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 491 and the convergence rate is O(338/n^3).

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 491 + O(1/n^338). Summing over all modes and applying the atoms convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
